One additive amendment only (below).<!----></p><!----><div></div><!----><!----><h2 dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!---->1. Scope</h2><!----><p dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!----><strong><!----><!---->In:<!----></strong><!----> payments UI + IPC document PDF · variations · cost loading (BoQ→activities) · progress recording &amp; earned value · S-curves &amp; project dashboard · daily reports · monthly progress report · AI infrastructure + first AI features (sequenced last — may slip without blocking the phase).<!----></p><!----><div></div><!----><p dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!----><strong><!----><!---->Out:<!----></strong><!----> actual-cost tracking / CPI (no data source in v1 — site expenditure entry is a future module) · retention release &amp; completion mechanics (4C) · baseline management UI (data-date updates exist; full baseline comparison later) · XER import (post-launch).<!----></p><!----><div></div><!----><!----><h2 dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!---->2. Cost–Schedule Integration (the differentiator — new domain law)</h2><!----><p dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!----><strong><!----><!---->Model:<!----></strong><!----> each BoQ item links to one or more activities with an allocation %. An activity's planned cost = Σ over linked items of round(item amount × allocation%, 2).<!----></p><!----><div></div><!----><div class=\"relative w-full group/table mb-5\"><div class=\"overflow-x-auto relative w-full scrollbar-hidden\"><table class=\"min-w-full text-sm text-left text-gray-500 w-fit dark:text-gray-400\"><thead class=\"text-gray-700 uppercase border-none dark:text-gray-400\"><tr class=\"border-none bg-[#f7f7f7] dark:bg-[#F7F7F70F]\"><th scope=\"col\" class=\"px-3! py-1.5!\" style=\"\"><div class=\"gap-1.5 text-left\"><div class=\"break-normal shrink-0\"><!----><!---->ID<!----></div></div></th><th scope=\"col\" class=\"px-3! py-1.5!\" style=\"\"><div class=\"gap-1.5 text-left\"><div class=\"break-normal shrink-0\"><!----><!---->Rule<!----></div></div></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class=\"border-b-1 border-[#d1d5dc] dark:border-gray-850\"><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!---->BR-CS1<!----></td><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!---->Allocations per BoQ item must sum to exactly 100% (decimal-exact) — validation error otherwise. An item may be <!----><strong><!----><!---->unlinked<!----></strong><!----> (allowed; warning only).<!----></td></tr><tr class=\"border-b-1 border-[#d1d5dc] dark:border-gray-850\"><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!---->BR-CS2<!----></td><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!---->Activity planned cost distributes <!----><strong><!----><!---->linearly across its working days<!----></strong><!----> (CPM ES..EF from the latest schedule run). Milestones (duration 0) carry no spread.<!----></td></tr><tr class=\"border-b-1 border-[#d1d5dc] dark:border-gray-850\"><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!---->BR-CS3<!----></td><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!---->Daily planned values are kept full-precision internally; period buckets and cumulative values round HALF_UP to 2dp at output.<!----></td></tr><tr class=\"border-b-1 border-[#d1d5dc] dark:border-gray-850\"><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!---->BR-CS4<!----></td><td class=\"px-3! py-3! border-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-[#EBEBEB] dark:border-[#FFFFFF0D] max-w-[500px] min-w-[180px]\" style=\"\"><!----><!----><strong><!----><!---->Coverage<!----></strong><!----> = linked BoQ value ÷ total BoQ value. Trial mode: 30 days from first run, same tier structure.<!----></li></ul><!----><div></div><!----><p dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!----><strong><!----><!---->Grace &amp; clock tampering:<!----></strong><!----></p><!----><!----><ul dir=\"auto\"><li class=\"text-start\"><!----> <!----><!----><!----><!----><!---->Last-known-good state + <!----><code class=\"cursor-pointer codespan\">expires + 30d<!----></code><!----> grace works fully offline<!----></li><li class=\"text-start\"><!----> <!----><!----><!----><!----><!---->Clock rollback detection: monotonic \"last seen\" timestamp stored in both license cache and app DB — if current time &lt; last-seen − 1 day, flag &amp; freeze trial (not paid licenses) until online check<!----></li><li class=\"text-start\"><!----> <!----><!----><!----><!----><!---->Trial design: honest because eviction-proof — the data is <!----><em><!----><!---->always<!----></em><!----> yours<!----></li></ul><!----><div></div><!----><!----><h2 dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!---->3. Payments &amp; Fulfillment</h2><!----><p dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!----><strong><!----><!---->International:<!----></strong><!----> merchant-of-record — <!----><strong><!----><!---->Paddle<!----></strong><!----> (primary; handles global VAT, payouts to regions where Stripe is awkward). Fallback: Lemon Squeezy.\n<!----><strong><!----><!---->Regional (Egypt/Gulf):<!----></strong><!----> bank transfer → you issue the license manually through a <!----><strong><!----><!---->small admin tool<!----></strong><!----> (WO-61): paste customer details + plan + machine fingerprint → outputs signed <!----><code class=\"cursor-pointer codespan\">.lic<!----></code><!----> file. Same Ed25519 key as the app verifies with.\n<!----><strong><!----><!---->Key custody:<!----></strong><!----> the signing private key lives <!----><strong><!----><!---->only<!----></strong><!----> on a dedicated machine/USB, never in the repo, never on the dev machine that runs the agent. One person's discipline here is the whole DRM. (You are a one-man company; this is your castle wall.)<!----></p><!----><div></div><!----><p dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!----><strong><!----><!---->Key generation ritual (WO-60):<!----></strong><!----> generate Ed25519 keypair on the dedicated machine → embed public key in app source → keep private key offline with a printed backup in a physically safe place. The printed backup is not paranoia; you sell this product for years.<!----></p><!----><div></div><!----><!----><h2 dir=\"auto\" class=\"svelte-4sys19\"><!----><!---->5.
